The proposed approach and feature extraction strategy outperform the evaluated comparative methods, achieving accuracies of 83.177% for resting-versus-mathematical task classification, 76.107% for resting-versus-memory task classification, and 83.432% for resting-versus-music task classification. The findings illustrate the efficacy of integrating signal processing with deep learning methodologies to discriminate resting from cognitive states utilizing EEG signals.

</details>


### [105] [Distributionally Robust Federated Learning with Multi-Source Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20501)
*Yingzhu Liu,Zhongkui Li,Pengcheng You,Ashish Cherukuri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对联邦学习中跨客户端混合不确定性以及客户端内部分布模糊性，构建了局部模糊集可容许混合的并集式全局模糊集，给出了客户端可分重构、高概率泛化保证及联邦算法与收敛性证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习从私有客户端数据训练共享模型时，数据生成分布可能存在差异，且真实客户端混合比例常未知，导致整体群分布难以指定。现有方法虽针对跨客户端混合不确定性采用最坏情形优化，但假设客户端分布估计准确；而基于有限样本的估计往往不可靠。因此需要同时处理跨客户端混合不确定性与客户端内部分布模糊性。

Method: 构造一个全局模糊集，定义为各局部模糊集的可容许混合之并集；该构造允许客户端特定的模糊半径，并具有客户端可分重构形式。基于该结构建立高概率的样本外性能保证。进一步针对惩罚式重构设计联邦算法，并在更温和的正则条件下证明其收敛性。

Result: 理论上获得了高概率样本外性能保证，并在较弱的正则条件下证明了联邦算法的收敛性；仿真实验验证了算法的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过将跨客户端混合不确定性与客户端内分布模糊性统一到并集式全局模糊集中，可在有限样本下获得更稳健的联邦学习性能保证，并给出可收敛的联邦求解算法。

Abstract: Federated learning trains a shared model from private client data. In practice, data-generating distributions may differ, and the true mixture across clients is often unknown, making the underlying group distribution difficult to specify. Existing approaches address cross-client mixture uncertainty by optimizing against the worst-case mixture, yet assume accurate client-wise distribution estimates. However, these estimates can be unreliable when based on finite samples. To handle both cross-client mixture uncertainty and within-client distributional ambiguity, we construct a global ambiguity set as the union of admissible mixtures of local ambiguity sets. The construction allows client-specific ambiguity radii and admits a client-wise separable reformulation. Leveraging this structure, we establish a high-probability out-of-sample performance guarantee. We further develop a federated algorithm for a penalty-based reformulation and prove its convergence under milder regularity conditions. Simulations validate the algorithm's effectiveness.

</details>


### [106] [Radio Frequency Detection and Classification of Microplastics in Water](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20507)
*Jaden Tolbert,Md Saiful Islam,Pingshan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种机器学习辅助的射频介电谱流式细胞术（DiSC）平台，可在水相环境中对微/纳塑料颗粒进行无标记检测与单颗粒分类，8类10 μm微塑料的宏平均F1、精确率和召回率均超过0.71，且PET在含盐载体中性能基本保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微/纳塑料（MPs/NPs）是普遍存在的环境污染物，其丰度不断上升并可能带来健康影响，亟需快速、无标记的检测方法。当颗粒尺寸降至低微米范围时，传统光学与光谱技术因通量受限或样品前处理复杂而面临挑战，因此需要发展新的检测与分类手段。

Method: 提出机器学习（ML）辅助的射频（RF）介电谱流式细胞术（DiSC）平台，用于微塑料的无标记检测与分类。将8种标称直径10 μm的微塑料颗粒悬浮于去离子水中，在0.2–9 GHz范围内的4个频率下进行表征；以载体介质为参考，测量RF散射参数（S参数）的变化，并据此训练监督式ML模型，实现材料分类，包括混合样品及含盐水环境中的微塑料识别。

Result: 对于去离子水中的8类微塑料，该方法的宏平均F1分数、精确率和召回率均超过0.71；在含3.3%和6.6%海盐的盐水载体介质中，PET的分类性能基本得以保持。

Conclusion: 结果证明，ML辅助RF DiSC用于水相环境中快速、单颗粒微塑料分类具有可行性。未来工作将聚焦于提升分类性能，途径包括改进RF校准、扩大频谱覆盖范围、增大训练数据集，并利用环境老化及生物污染的微塑料进行验证。

Abstract: Micro- and nano-plastic particles (MPs/NPs) are ubiquitous environmental contaminants whose increasing abundance and potential health impacts have created an urgent need for rapid, label-free detection methods. As particle size decreases to the low-micrometer range, conventional optical and spectroscopic techniques become increasingly challenging because of limited throughput and/or complex sample preparation. In this work, we present a machine learning (ML)-assisted radio-frequency (RF) dielectric spectroscopic cytometry (DiSC) platform for the label-free detection and classification of MPs. Eight types of $ 10 $ μm nominal-diameter MP particles suspended in deionized (DI) water were characterized at four frequencies spanning $ 0.2\text{-}9\text{ GHz} $. The measured alterations in RF scattering parameters (S-parameters), referenced to the carrier medium, were used to train supervised ML models for material classification, including the identification of MPs in mixed samples and saline-water environments. For eight MP classes suspended in DI water, the proposed method achieved macro-average F1-score, precision, and recall values exceeding $ 0.71 $. Furthermore, PET classification performance was largely maintained in saline carrier media containing $3.3\% $ and $ 6.6\% $ sea salt. These results demonstrate the feasibility of ML-assisted RF DiSC for rapid, single-particle MP classification in aqueous environments. Future work will focus on improving classification performance through enhanced RF calibration, increased spectral coverage, larger training datasets, and validation using environmentally aged and biologically contaminated microplastics.

</details>


### [107] [When EOS Tokens Disagree: Understanding Length Inflation in On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20511)
*Yuxiao Yang,Tianrun Yu,Shangzhe Li,Kaixiang Zhao,Xuchao Zhang,Chetan Bansal,Huaxiu Yao,Taylor W. Killian,Weitong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在线策略蒸馏（OPD）中的长度膨胀问题，指出基座学生与后训练教师在终止token上的不匹配是学生回复过长的重要原因，并提出将功能等价的EOS token视为共享语义终止动作来缓解该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPD中学生回复可能过度变长，甚至耗尽生成预算。作者希望找出这种长度膨胀的来源，并探究终止行为在训练过程中如何演变。

Method: 作者识别出基座学生与后训练教师之间存在"终止token不匹配"：即便声明的停止集合相同，两者也可能将停止概率放在不同的EOS token上。作者在Qwen3、Llama和Gemma上验证该现象，并对比仅对齐解码停止集合与将功能等价EOS token视为共享语义终止动作两种做法；同时在不同K2-Horizon训练阶段上分析OPD。

Result: 仅对齐解码停止集合不足以解决问题；将功能等价EOS token当作共享语义终止动作可显著缓解三个模型家族中由不匹配引起的长度膨胀。阶段分析显示终止偏好会随训练大幅变化，并在OPD后期出现一种超出终止对齐范围的独立长度膨胀。

Conclusion: 终止不匹配是OPD长度动态的一个重要但非全部来源。作者发布了包含所提终止处理修正的实现。

Abstract: We study length inflation in on-policy distillation (OPD), where student responses can become excessively long and even exhaust the generation budget. We identify \emph{termination-token mismatch} between base students and post-trained teachers as an important source of this behavior. Across Qwen3, Llama, and Gemma, the two models can place their stopping probability on different EOS tokens, even when their declared stopping sets are identical. This mismatch can suppress the student's preferred termination action without reliably transferring the teacher-preferred alternative. We show that aligning the decoding stopping set alone is insufficient, while treating functionally equivalent EOS tokens as a shared semantic stopping action substantially mitigates mismatch-induced length inflation across all three model families. To further understand how termination behavior evolves over training, we study OPD across different K2-Horizon training stages. This stage-wise analysis shows that termination preferences can shift substantially during training, while also revealing a distinct length inflation late in the OPD run that persists beyond termination alignment. Together, these results identify termination mismatch as an important, but not exhaustive, source of OPD length dynamics. We release an implementation incorporating the proposed termination-handling corrections.

</details>


### [108] [Parallelism, critical windows, and separations among diffusion language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20539)
*Sitan Chen,Liye Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次对masked、uniform、Gaussian三种扩散大语言模型(dLLM)范式的并行能力进行细粒度理论比较，证明uniform与Gaussian扩散的采样前向次数可随分布的内在复杂度（对偶总相关）缩放，并在随机经验测度族上给出平方根级与线性级的并行性分离，确立了三者间首个可证明的并行性差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: dLLM的核心卖点是相比自回归模型能更高效并行生成文本，但不同扩散范式（masked、uniform、Gaussian）在并行性上的优劣缺乏原则性理解，已有的只在masked扩散上证明了可随内在复杂度缩放的采样效率，三者间的比较仍是空白。

Method: 理论分析：为uniform与Gaussian扩散构造采样算法，证明其前向次数可随对偶总相关（dual total correlation，可远小于上下文长度的内在复杂度度量）缩放；针对一类随机经验测度族，分别推导uniform/Gaussian扩散所需前向次数的上下界，并构造相应近似score oracle分析masked扩散的下界，从而建立三者并行性的可证明分离。

Result: uniform与Gaussian扩散可在与对偶总相关成比例的前向次数内采样，此前该性质仅对masked扩散已知；在一类随机经验测度上，uniform或Gaussian扩散采样需且仅需Õ(√d)次前向，而存在近似score oracle使masked扩散需要Õ(d)次前向，首次给出三类主流dLLM范式的并行性可证明分离。

Conclusion: 与"masked扩散因必须确定token取值而更难并行"的流行直觉相反，该分离的根源在于masked扩散采样中的关键窗口(critical windows)渐近地比uniform与Gaussian扩散更窄，从而导致其并行效率更低。

Abstract: A popular selling point of diffusion large language models (dLLMs) is their capacity for parallelism: the ability to generate sequences of text far more efficiently than autoregressive models, which require one forward pass per token. Yet among the many competing paradigms for dLLMs, from masked to uniform to Gaussian diffusion, principled understanding of how these different proposals compare in parallelism remains limited. In this work, we initiate a fine-grained comparison of the capacity for parallelism among these three leading approaches and prove the following:
  - Uniform and Gaussian diffusion can sample in a number of forward passes which scales with the dual total correlation of the underlying distribution, a measure of intrinsic complexity which can be much smaller than the context length. Previously, it was only known how to achieve this using masked diffusion.
  - For a certain family of random empirical measures, we show that $\widetildeΘ(\sqrt{d})$ forward passes are necessary and sufficient to sample using uniform or Gaussian diffusion, yet there exist approximate score oracles for which $\widetildeΩ(d)$ forward passes are needed for masked diffusion. This establishes the first provable separation in parallelism between the three prevailing dLLM paradigms.
  Contrary to popular intuition that masked diffusions are harder to parallelize because they must commit to token values, the latter separation instead comes from the fact that the critical windows in masked diffusion sampling are asymptotically narrower than those in uniform and Gaussian diffusion sampling.

</details>


### [109] [Mitigating Retaliatory Algorithmic Collusion in Repeated Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20548)
*Karthik Sivachandran,Rohan Paleja*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过将Q学习中的合谋现象与经典简单惩罚码（SPC）理论建立联系，提出奖励塑形框架CURB，通过惩罚策略的条件依赖信号来消除合谋均衡，在Bertrand和Cournot重复博弈中显著减少合谋。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习智能体在重复博弈中仅通过最大化自身奖励就可能收敛到类似显式合谋的超竞争结果，且无需通信或共享设计。现有缓解方法多局限于特定经济场景（如双边平台、拍卖），缺乏面向一般重复博弈的干预设计。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 作者首先形式化地建立前期Q学习合谋实证观察与经典简单惩罚码（SPC）理论之间的联系，证明任何非平凡SPC都会在智能体策略中诱导可量化的条件依赖，可通过智能体在合作历史与背叛历史下动作分布的总变差距离（TV距离）检测。基于此提出CURB（通过奖励塑形与信念注入解除合谋）框架，在Q学习过程中惩罚该TV距离信号，并理论保证将动力系统的任何SPC不动点转化为平凡不动点，从而排除由惩罚威胁维持的合谋均衡。

Result: 在Bertrand和Cournot竞争重复博弈中，CURB显著降低了Q学习智能体的合谋程度；进一步在Bertrand竞争的深度Q网络（DQN）智能体上验证了CURB的有效性，表明该机制可推广到表格型Q学习之外。

Conclusion: CURB提供了一种面向一般重复博弈的合谋缓解机制，通过理论驱动的TV距离惩罚消除了SPC惩罚威胁支撑的合谋均衡，并在表格型和深度强化学习设定中均表现出有效性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning agents trained to maximize their own reward in repeated interactions can converge to supra-competitive outcomes resembling explicit collusion, without communication or shared design. Existing mitigation approaches are largely tied to specific economic settings, like two-sided platforms and auctions, leaving open how to design interventions for general repeated games. We address this gap by formalizing the connection between empirical observations from prior work on Q-learning collusion and classical theory of Simple Penal Codes (SPCs). We show any non-trivial SPC induces a quantifiable conditional dependence in agents' policies, detectable via the total variation distance between an agent's action distributions across cooperation and defection histories. Building on this connection, we propose CURB (Collusion Unwinding via Reward shaping and Belief injection), a reward-shaping framework that penalizes this Total Variation (TV) distance signal during Q-learning and is guaranteed to convert any SPC fixed point of the dynamics into a trivial one, thus precluding collusive equilibria sustained by punishment threats. Empirically, CURB substantially reduces collusion by Q-learning agents in both Bertrand and Cournot Competition Repeated Games. We further demonstrate that CURB extends to deep Q-network agents in Bertrand competition, suggesting the mechanism generalizes beyond tabular Q-learning.

</details>


### [110] [CrystalMO-TuRBO: Multi-Objective Trust-Region Bayesian Optimization for High-precision Joint Crystal Structure Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20592)
*Joseph Agada,Yishu Wang,Arpan Biswas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CrystalMO-TuRBO，一种多目标信赖域贝叶斯优化架构，将X射线与中子衍射差异建模为独立目标，采用两阶段优化实现联合晶体结构精修。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统精修方法（最小二乘、似然优化）依赖局部搜索，在非凸、含噪、参数强相关的能量景观中表现不佳；多模态（X射线与中子）联合精修因灵敏度互补但相互竞争，通常通过标量化目标结合，需人工加权且易得到次优解。

Method: 将X射线与中子衍射失配建模为两个独立目标并转化为归一化最大化问题；采用两阶段策略：第一阶段通过并行信赖域贝叶斯优化在多个标量化下进行全局探索以定位有潜力的参数区域，第二阶段在收缩区域内进行局部精修以获得高精度解，从而显式分离全局搜索与精细优化。

Result: 在单晶Ho2Ti2O7的实验X射线与中子衍射数据上评估，相比经典精修方法和贝叶斯优化基线，在收敛性、鲁棒性和参数精度上均有提升。

Conclusion: 多目标信赖域贝叶斯优化为多模态联合晶体结构精修提供了有效框架，可缓解人工加权与局部最优问题，在单晶焦绿石体系上验证了其精度与鲁棒性优势。

Abstract: Crystal structure refinement is a fundamental inverse problem in materials characterization, where structural parameters are optimized to reproduce experimental diffraction data. Conventional approaches, such as least-squares and likelihood-based optimization, rely on local search and often struggle with non-convex, noisy, and highly correlated parameter landscapes, particularly when integrating multiple diffraction modalities. Joint refinement of X-ray and neutron data is especially challenging due to their complementary but competing sensitivities, which are typically combined through scalarized objectives requiring manual weighting and leading to suboptimal solutions. We propose CrystalMO-TuRBO, a multi-objective trust region Bayesian optimization architecture for joint crystal structure refinement. The method models X-ray and neutron discrepancies as separate objectives and transforms the problem into a normalized maximization setting. A two-phase optimization strategy is introduced: Phase 1 performs global exploration using parallel trust-region Bayesian optimization across multiple scalarizations to identify promising regions of the parameter space, while Phase 2 conducts localized refinement within a shrinking region to achieve high-precision solutions. This design explicitly separates global search from fine-grained optimization, addressing the unique accuracy requirements of refinement tasks. We evaluate the proposed method on experimentally collected X-ray and neutron diffraction data from single-crystal Ho2Ti2O7. Results demonstrate improved convergence, robustness, and parameter precision compared to classical refinement methods and Bayesian optimization baselines on refinement of a single-crystal pyrochlore material system.

</details>


### [111] [Recursive Quantum Long Short-Term Memory for Stable Short-Horizon Temperature Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20594)
*Mu-En Lee,Yen-Ku Liu,Samuel Yen-Chi Chen,Yun-Cheng Tsai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文比较递归式QLSTM与标准QLSTM在日最低/最高气温一步预测任务上的表现，使用多伦多气象数据与20个随机种子，在8/16/32天输入窗口下评估。递归模型更快收敛、误差更低、泛化差距更小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子长短期记忆（QLSTM）用变分量子电路扩展循环序列学习，但其优化行为对随机初始化和时间上下文高度敏感，性能不稳定。

Method: 提出并评估一种递归式QLSTM架构，与标准QLSTM对比，用于日最低和最高气温的一步预测。使用多伦多每日气象观测数据、相同训练设置，在8、16、32天输入窗口及20个随机种子上比较收敛速度、预测精度和泛化能力。

Result: 递归模型一致地更早达到接近最优的测试损失，降低平均绝对误差（MAE）和均方根误差（RMSE），并表现出更小的泛化差距。

Conclusion: 递归量子特征变换可提升紧凑型混合量子-经典时序模型的稳定性和样本外性能。

Abstract: Quantum long short-term memory (QLSTM) models extend recurrent sequence learning with variational quantum circuits, but their optimization behavior can vary substantially across random initializations and temporal contexts. This paper evaluates a recursive QLSTM architecture against a standard QLSTM for one-step-ahead prediction of daily minimum and maximum temperature. Using daily weather observations from Toronto and identical training settings, we compare convergence, predictive accuracy, and generalization across input windows of 8, 16, and 32 days over 20 random seeds. The recursive model consistently reaches a near-optimal test loss earlier, reduces mean absolute error and root mean squared error, and exhibits a smaller generalization gap. These results indicate that recursive quantum feature transformations can improve stability and out-of-sample performance for compact hybrid quantum--classical temporal models.

</details>


### [112] [COIN-GP: Cooperative Online Learning in Networked Distributed Systems with Partial Measurements via Gaussian Process Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20598)
*Zewen Yang,Xiaobing Dai,Zhenxiao Yin,Hang Zhao,Zhijun Li,C. C. Chan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对分布式传感网络中仅有部分状态观测的场景，提出基于观测器的动态协同学习框架，结合在线分布式高斯过程回归，联合估计系统状态与部分未知动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在配备传感器的分布式网络中，需同时估计系统状态与部分未知动态，但实际中往往只能获得部分状态观测，且高斯过程模型本身存在不足，现有分布式GP方法难以在测量不完整与模型欠缺下实现准确估计。

Method: 提出基于观测器的动态协同学习框架，融入在线分布式高斯过程回归；设计新的数据采集策略，并给出确保数据采集可行的理论条件；利用GP的确定性误差界推导涵盖状态估计与模型估计的误差上界。

Result: 在测量不完整和GP模型欠缺的情况下仍能实现准确估计；理论条件保证数据采集可行；给出状态与模型估计的误差上界；仿真实验表明该方法优于现有基于分布式GP的方法。

Conclusion: 所提观测器驱动的在线分布式GP协同学习框架可有效解决部分观测下的状态与未知动态联合估计问题，并具备理论保证与实验验证的优越性。

Abstract: In this paper, we tackle the problem of jointly estimating the system states and partially unknown dynamics within distributed sensor-equipped networks, particularly in scenarios where only partial state observations are available. To address this issue, we propose an observer-based dynamic cooperative learning framework incorporating online distributed Gaussian Process (GP) regression, which enables accurate estimation despite incomplete in measurements and deficient GP models. In addition, a novel data collection strategy is introduced, with theoretical conditions ensuring feasible data acquisition. Moreover, we also derive an error upper bound encompassing state estimation and model estimation, leveraging the deterministic error bounds of GPs. Empirical simulations demonstrate the superiority of our approach compared to existing distributed GP-based methods.

</details>


### [113] [Multi-center Medical Data Mining with FL-Net - A One-stop Shop for Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20650)
*Simon Süwer,Julian Klemm,Elisa Acitelli,Mathieu Almeida,Lucia Altucci,Zsolt Bagyura,Michelangela Barbieri,Zsolt-Zoltán Bedő,Rosaria Benedetti,Béla Bihari,Csongor Csalóka,Lucia Dicunta,Stanislav Ehrlich,Bjoern M. Eskofier,Sándor-József Fejér,Georg Fröwis,Walter Hötzendorfer,Alexandra Kautzky-Willer,Jens Johann Georg Lohmann,Marianna Maranghi,Lorenzo Marconi,Rudolf Mayer,Wouter Leonard Megchelenbrink,Monika Moga,Adham Mottalib,Sanjeev Mehta,Madeleine Müller,Thomas Nyström,Balázs-Attila Orbán,Paul O'Toole,Giuseppe Paolisso,Paolo Parini,Matteo Pedrelli,Enrico Petrillo,Philipp Poindl,Niklas Probul,Anastasia Pustozerova,Tanja Šarčević,Lukas Weilguny,Jan Baumbach,Andreas Maier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出FL-Net——一个满足临床联邦研究五项需求的端到端框架，整合数据协调、发现、披露控制、安全版本化工具与容器化联邦工作流，并在MIMIC与US-130上完成验证，支持多达50个并发客户端。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习可实现无需共享患者级数据的协作训练，但多数研究仍停留在仿真阶段；现有14个FL框架经文献归纳的五项需求检验后，均无法完全满足临床研究的实际要求。

Method: 基于文献推导的五项需求，先对14个现有FL框架进行对比分析；进而设计FL-Net框架，将模块化数据协调、数据发现、披露控制、安全构建的版本化FL-Net-Tools以及容器化联邦工作流执行集成到持久化网络中。

Result: 通过数据协调、跨MIMIC与US-130的跨研究患者发现，以及支持最多50个并发客户端的可复现、可审计联邦工作流，对FL-Net的端到端能力进行了评估。

Conclusion: FL-Net为可互操作、可复现且隐私保护的多中心临床研究提供了实用基础；该框架正于dAIbetes与Microb-AI-ome等EU项目中开发，将覆盖9国10家医院逾80万名患者，涵盖纵向与单时点数据。

Abstract: Federated learning enables collaborative training without sharing patient-level data, but most studies remain simulations. Based on five requirements derived from the literature, we analyzed 14 FL frameworks and found that none fully satisfied these requirements. We present FL-Net, a novel federated clinical research framework to fulfill all requirements. It integrates modular data harmonization, data discovery, disclosure control, securely built versioned FL-Net-Tools and containerized federated workflow execution into a persistent network. It enables the re-use of harmonized data and workflows across studies. FL-Net's end-to-end capabilities were evaluated through harmonization, cross-study patient discovery across MIMIC and US-130, and reproducible, audited federated workflows with up to 50 concurrent clients. FL-Net is being developed within the dAIbetes and Microb-AI-ome EU projects and will cover over 800,000 patients across 10 hospitals in 9 countries covering longitudinal and single point in time data, FL-Net provides a practical foundation for interoperable, reproducible, and privacy-preserving multicenter clinical research.

</details>


### [114] [Epidemiological Causal Graph Identification: Challenges, Identifiability and Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20676)
*Sambit Mishra,Yingying Wang,Christine K. Johnson,Urbashi Mitra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究混合数据类型（序数与指数族）有向无环图中的因果方向可识别性问题，证明了序数节点与指数族节点之间的边方向对一般参数值具有分布可识别性，并提出了基于评分穷举搜索与DAGMA掩码连续优化的计算框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果可识别性研究主要集中于连续变量与加性噪声模型，忽视了包含序数尺度、计数与连续测量的混合数据集，这类数据在实际中广泛存在。

Method: 将节点建模为服从序数分布（通过有序logit模型）或单参数指数族分布；证明序数与指数族节点间边方向的分布可识别性；提出基于评分的穷举搜索以及使用DAGMA的掩码连续优化框架以处理更大规模图。

Result: 理论结果将先前的序数-泊松结论推广到更广泛的指数族；数值实验验证了理论，能够在马尔可夫等价类内恢复在经典结构方程模型下不可识别的边方向。

Conclusion: 该研究扩展了混合离散-连续数据类型下因果发现的可识别性理论，并提供可扩展的计算方法，实现比经典SEM更强的方向识别能力。

Abstract: Causal discovery from observational data is fundamental to statistics and machine learning, yet determining causal direction without interventions necessitates structural assumptions. Existing identifiability research primarily focuses on continuous variables under additive noise models, often neglecting mixed datasets containing ordinal scales, counts, and continuous measurements. This paper investigates causal discovery in Directed Acyclic Graphs (DAGs) where nodes follow either an ordinal distribution (via an ordered logit model) or a regular one-parameter exponential family distribution. We prove that the edge direction between an ordinal and an exponential family node is distributionally identifiable for generic parameter values. Our findings generalize previous Ordinal-Poisson results to the broader exponential family. Computationally, we introduce a score-based exhaustive search and a masked continuous optimization framework using DAGMA for larger graphs. Numerical results validate the theory, recovering edge orientations within a Markov equivalence class that are unidentifiable under classical structural equation models.

</details>


### [115] [RISC-V and machine learning: a survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20677)
*Shriman Keshri,Apparna Singh,Chinmaya Kumar Palo,Shreya Adya,Subhankar Mishra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了 RISC-V 指令集架构在机器学习中的应用现状，基于近期研究提出统一分类体系、性能与设计权衡对比及软件工具链成熟度评估，并给出四个未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源处理器架构与机器学习的交叉融合，催生了对可定制、高效且易获取硬件的需求，因此有必要系统梳理 RISC-V 在机器学习领域的能力、挑战与发展方向。

Method: 综述性分析：覆盖学术与商业实现、软件框架及实际应用；从指令集扩展、核心实现、编译器优化到部署策略评估 RISC-V 机器学习生态，并构建统一分类体系与比较分析。

Result: 在能效、专用指令开发和框架集成方面取得进展；同时存在标准化不足、验证复杂度高和生态碎片化等挑战。

Conclusion: 提出四个研究方向：专用神经处理扩展、自适应与模块化处理器架构、安全框架、高能效多域架构，为 RISC-V 作为下一代机器学习系统基础平台提供路线图。

Abstract: The intersection of open-source processor architectures and machine learning is driving the demand for customizable, efficient, and accessible hardware. This survey examines the state of the RISC-V ISA in machine learning applications, analyzing current capabilities, challenges, and future directions based on recent research. The analysis covers academic and commercial implementations, software frameworks, and real-world applications. The RISC-V machine learning ecosystem is evaluated, from instruction set extensions and core implementations to compiler optimizations and deployment strategies. Key contributions include a unified taxonomy of RISC-V ML implementations, a comparative analysis of performance and design trade-offs, an evaluation of software toolchain maturity, and the identification of emerging trends in instruction set extensions and specialized accelerators. Findings reveal progress in energy efficiency, specialized instruction development, and framework integration, while highlighting challenges in standardization, verification complexity, and ecosystem fragmentation. The analysis proposes four research directions to address current limitations: specialized neural processing extensions, adaptive and modular processor architectures, security frameworks, and energy-efficient multi-domain architectures. These directions provide a roadmap for advancing RISC-V as a foundational platform for next-generation machine learning systems.

</details>


### [116] [Don't Mask the Environment: Observation Supervision Changes How Agents Explore Under RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20715)
*Juzheng Zhang,Disha Makhija,Manoj Ghuhan Arivazhagan,Vinayshekhar Bannihatti Kumar,Rashmi Gangadharaiah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ActObs方法，在SFT中同时对轨迹中已有的观测token进行监督，虽不增加数据/参数/序列长度/前向次数，但能为后续GRPO强化学习提供更优初始化，提升pass@k并保持更高策略熵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准SFT仅对agent生成的动作token计算损失，将环境观测仅作上下文而非预测目标。作者质疑这一惯例是否为后续RL的最佳初始化，希望让策略学习动作后果建模。

Method: ActObs在SFT中额外监督每条轨迹中已存在的观测token（观测由环境产生，部署时无需生成）。方法不增加数据、参数、序列token或前向计算量；随后用GRPO进行强化学习，并在Qwen3-4B与Qwen3-8B上评估。

Result: SFT后两种方法表现相近，但GRPO后出现分化。4B模型上，ActObs的GRPO在所有采样预算下pass@k均更高（Terminal-Bench 2.0）；8B上以部分pass@1换取更高pass@k（pass@16 +3.4pp）并解决更多不同任务。跨域代码编辑aider-polyglot上4B模型pass@1 +4.2pp（任务在SFT和RL中均未见过）。ActObs在RL中保持更高熵、所需策略移动更少，最终策略更接近SFT初始化。

Conclusion: 联合监督可防止单侧专业化，避免动作-观测梯度正交后观测梯度残留导致环境预测能力低于基模型的问题，从而保留后果预测能力并为下游探索做好准备。

Abstract: Agent trajectories record what an agent does and what happens next. Yet standard supervised fine-tuning (SFT) applies loss only to agent-authored action tokens, using environment observations as context but not as prediction targets. We ask whether this convention provides the best initialization for subsequent reinforcement learning. We introduce ActObs, which also supervises the observation tokens already present in each trajectory. Although deployed agents never generate observations, learning to predict them encourages the policy to model action consequences without adding data, parameters, sequence tokens, or forward passes. The methods perform similarly after SFT but diverge after GRPO. On Qwen3-4B, GRPO from ActObs achieves higher pass@k at every evaluated sampling budget than its action-only counterpart on Terminal-Bench 2.0. On Qwen3-8B, it trades some pass@1 reliability for higher pass@k (+3.4 pp at pass@16) and solves more distinct tasks. The advantage extends to cross-domain code editing on aider-polyglot (+4.2 pp at pass@1 at 4B), whose tasks are unseen during SFT and RL. ActObs retains more entropy during RL while requiring less policy movement, leaving the final policy closer to its SFT initialization. Our analysis traces this difference to SFT: action and observation gradients rapidly become orthogonal, while action-only training leaves a large residual observation gradient and degrades environment prediction below the base model. Joint supervision prevents this one-sided specialization, preserving consequence prediction and preparing the policy for downstream exploration.

</details>


### [117] [Video DeltaNet: A Video-Native Hybrid Attention for Livestream Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20744)
*Haocheng Xi,Yiming Xie,Hexu Zhao,Yiwen Zhang,Michael Liu,Thomas Creavin,Kurt Keutzer,Xiuyu Li,Zhaoyang Lv,Chenfeng Xu,Haiwen Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Video DeltaNet (VDN)，将局部 Softmax 注意力与双向线性记忆分支（Video Delta Attention，VDA）结合用于长程视频上下文建模，并实例化为 VDN-H3。在八卡 NVIDIA B200 上，14.3 秒 768p 视频的 DiT 去噪仅需 6.70 秒，相比 50 步稠密 H3 基线实现 14.5 倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频扩散模型在去噪过程中需反复处理长时空 token 序列，注意力机制成为主要计算瓶颈。线性注意力虽在近期大语言模型中被广泛采用，但直接迁移到视频模型会损失高质量生成所需的细粒度交互。因此需要一种兼顾长程建模效率与细粒度生成质量的新注意力方案。

Method: 提出 Video DeltaNet（VDN），核心是混合注意力架构：局部 Softmax 注意力负责细粒度交互，双向线性记忆分支负责长程视频上下文。线性分支引入 Video Delta Attention（VDA），以帧为单位、联合该帧的空间 token 更新记忆。两分支通过独立的输出投影与可学习门控进行校准；并设计分阶段教师对齐（staged teacher-alignment）策略，将新通路渐进式引入预训练模型。在 MiniMax H3 上实例化为 VDN-H3，将混合注意力应用于 video-to-video 交互，而涉及文本或音频的交互仍保留 Softmax。此外结合八步蒸馏与优化的 SGLang 服务栈。

Result: VDN-H3 在八块 NVIDIA B200 GPU 上完成 14.3 秒、768p 视频的 DiT 去噪仅需 6.70 秒，相比同等 GPU 数量下 50 步稠密 H3 基线取得 14.5 倍加速，同时借助混合设计保持生成质量。

Conclusion: 通过局部 Softmax 与双向线性记忆的混合注意力设计、逐帧联合空间 token 的记忆更新机制以及分阶段教师对齐，VDN 有效缓解了视频扩散模型的注意力计算瓶颈，在保持高质量生成的同时实现显著推理加速，为长视频高效生成提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Video diffusion models repeatedly process long spatiotemporal token sequences during denoising, making attention a major computational bottleneck. Linear attention offers an appealing alternative and has been widely adopted in recent large language models, but directly applying it to video models often fails to preserve the fine-grained interactions required for high-quality generation. We present Video DeltaNet (VDN), which combines local Softmax attention with bidirectional linear memory for long-range video context. Its linear branch introduces Video Delta Attention (VDA), which updates memory once per frame by jointly incorporating its spatial tokens. Separate output projections and learnable gates calibrate the two branches, while a staged teacher-alignment recipe progressively introduces the new pathway into pretrained models. We instantiate VDN on MiniMax H3, applying the hybrid to video-to-video interactions while retaining Softmax for interactions involving text or audio. With eight-step distillation and an optimized SGLang serving stack, VDN-H3 completes DiT denoising for a 14.3-second, 768p video in 6.70 seconds on eight NVIDIA B200 GPUs, corresponding to a 14.5x speedup over the 50-step dense H3 baseline on the same GPU count.

</details>


### [118] [Calibrated RF-Fingerprinting Under Interference With Heterogeneous Transmission Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20765)
*Tariq Abdul-Quddoos,Xiangfang Li,Lijun Qian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将RF指纹识别从单发射机场景扩展到同信道干扰场景，将其建模为多标签分类问题，采用1D CNN并引入校准机制以保证假阴性率上界，在POWDER 5G实测数据（Wi-Fi、4G LTE、5G NR）上验证，准确率达73%–97%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RF指纹识别研究几乎只考虑单一发射机发射的场景，忽略了现实中多信号在时间和频率上重叠、相互干扰的情况，导致其实际适用性受限。

Method: 1) 将存在同信道干扰的多发射机识别问题形式化为多标签分类问题；2) 采用一维卷积神经网络（1D CNN）进行建模；3) 对模型进行校准，为标签概率推导置信阈值，并对平均假阴性数量的上界提供理论保证。

Result: 基于POWDER 5G试验床的真实数据，对发射802.11a(Wi-Fi)、4G LTE和5G NR波形的设备进行验证。校准后准确率视信道条件在73%至97%之间；在不同平均假阴性上界下校准可获得约等于(1 - 校准假阴性率)的micro recall分数，且校准对分布外干扰具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该方法在高竞争的真实无线环境中展现出良好潜力，能够用于识别共存干扰下的多个发射机，并在不遗漏真实频谱政策违规方面提供一定置信度保障。

Abstract: Radio Frequency(RF)-Fingerprinting is a spectrum monitoring technique that identifies specific transmitters based on hardware impairments imprinted within the emitted signal. Although widely researched, studies almost exclusively consider scenarios where only one transmitter is emitting at a time, limiting real world applicability. In this work, we further the study of RF-Fingerprinting by considering co-channel interference, with multiple emitted signals interfering with each other, overlapping in time and frequency. Specifically, we formulate this problem as a multi-label classification problem and employ a 1D convolutional neural network (CNN). Furthermore, the models are calibrated such that the confidence thresholds for the label probabilities are derived, with guarantees on the upper bound on the average number of False Negatives, providing a degree of confidence in not missing a true spectrum policy violation. The proposed method is validated using real world data from the POWDER 5G testbed on devices transmitting 802.11a(Wi-Fi), 4G LTE, and 5G NR waveforms. The results show accuracy as high as 97% and as low as 73% after calibration depending on channel conditions. Also calibrating for various average false negatives upper bounds achieves micro recall scores of approximately (1 - calibrated false negatives) with the calibration robust to out-of-distribution interference, demonstrating the potential of the proposed method in a realistic high contention wireless environment

</details>


### [119] [PosteriorBench: From Point Estimates to Posterior Matching in Evaluating Generative Inverse Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20794)
*Jiachen Yao,Zi-Siang Hsu,Xi Deng,Aditi Gupta,Xin Ju,Sally M Benson,Gege Wen,Anima Anandkumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 PosteriorBench，一个用于评估生成式逆问题求解器分布精度的基准，包含四个物理逆问题、高保真参考后验以及五项后验评估指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式逆问题评估主要关注能否产生单一合理重构，忽略病态问题存在多解的情形。此类方法可能点态精度高，却因模式崩溃、过度自信不确定性或对不相容解求平均而无法捕捉真实后验。

Method: 构建 PosteriorBench 基准，涵盖 Darcy 流反演、Poisson 源恢复、碳捕集与封存、光传输材料推断四个物理逆问题；用拒绝采样和 MCMC 等重型但成熟的流程构造高保真参考后验；配套五项后验评估指标：后验均值误差、后验标准差误差、最大均值差异、切片 Wasserstein 距离、径向平均功率谱误差；覆盖稀疏感知、低分辨率观测、非线性前向模型、不同噪声水平与多峰先验，并采用统一管线进行分布匹配与不确定性量化。

Result: 实验揭示当前求解器存在显著分布匹配差距；神经算子可提升分辨率鲁棒性；引导权重与生成噪声是后验方差校准的关键。

Conclusion: 该基准将评估重点从单点重构转向全后验分布精度，为生成式逆问题求解器提供统一的分布匹配与不确定性量化评估框架。

Abstract: Generative models are increasingly used to solve scientific inverse problems, but existing evaluations still focus primarily on whether a method can produce a single plausible reconstruction. This is insufficient for ill-posed problems, where multiple solutions may be consistent with the same sparse or noisy observations. In these settings, a method can achieve strong pointwise accuracy while still failing to capture the true posterior through mode collapse, overconfident uncertainty, or averaging incompatible solutions. We introduce PosteriorBench, a benchmark for evaluating the distributional accuracy of generative inverse solvers. PosteriorBench evaluates four physics-based inverse problems: Darcy flow inversion, Poisson source recovery, carbon capture and storage, and light transport material inference. For each task, we construct high-fidelity reference posteriors using computationally heavy but established procedures such as rejection sampling and Markov chain Monte Carlo, enabling direct assessment of whether solvers recover the full set of solutions rather than the single best sample. We pair these references with a five-metric posterior evaluation suite: posterior-mean error, posterior-standard-deviation error, maximum mean discrepancy, sliced Wasserstein distance, and radially averaged power-spectrum error. These metrics assess pointwise accuracy, marginal uncertainty, distributional alignment, and global frequency fidelity. The benchmark spans sparse sensing, low-resolution observations, nonlinear forward models, varying noise levels, and multimodal priors, with a unified pipeline for distribution matching and uncertainty quantification. Our experiments reveal substantial distribution-matching gaps across current solvers, while showing that neural operators improve resolution robustness, and guidance weights and generation noise are key to posterior-variance calibration.

</details>


### [120] [Score Centering Stabilizes Off-policy Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20807)
*Martin Marek,Max Ryabinin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出大模型强化学习中训练-推理不匹配（TIM）导致的训练不稳定主要源于"漂移"（drift）——训练与推理引擎间随每步训练累积的持续偏差，并提出一种加性的"score centering"校正项来消除漂移，在0.6B至30B模型上匹配或超越重要性采样方法，且可与重要性采样组合使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的强化学习对训练引擎与推理引擎间的微小差异（即训练-推理不匹配，TIM）极为敏感。然而完全消除TIM不切实际，因为这会严重牺牲rollout效率。因此需要一种在保留TIM的前提下仍能稳定RL训练的方法，作者旨在揭示TIM下训练不稳定的根本原因并提出针对性校正。

Method: 作者将TIM下RL的不稳定性归因于"漂移"：即训练与推理引擎之间随每个训练步累积的持续偏差。基于此推导出一个加性的"score centering"校正项，通过抵消漂移来稳定RL训练。由于该校正项是加性的，它还可以与重要性采样（importance sampling）方法组合使用。

Result: 在0.6B至30B参数规模的模型训练中，score centering单独使用即可匹配或超越量化条件下的重要性采样方法，且随着不匹配程度加重，优势进一步扩大。在staleness（陈旧度）实验中，score centering与重要性采样的组合优于纯重要性采样基线。

Conclusion: TIM下RL的不稳定性主要源于训练与推理引擎间累积的漂移偏差；通过加性的score centering校正抵消漂移即可有效稳定训练。该方法无需消除TIM，兼顾了rollout效率，且与重要性采样正交互补，为缓解训练-推理不匹配提供了简洁有效的方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) of large language models is notoriously sensitive to small differences between training and inference engines, often referred to as the training-inference mismatch (TIM). However, completely eliminating TIM is impractical, as it would come at a major cost to rollout efficiency. In this paper, we show that the instability of RL under TIM is primarily caused by drift: a persistent bias between training and inference engines that accumulates with every training step. We derive an additive "score centering" correction term that stabilizes RL under TIM by canceling drift. When training models from 0.6B to 30B parameters, score centering alone matches or outperforms methods based on importance sampling under quantization, with the gap growing as the mismatch becomes more severe. Because the correction is additive, score centering also composes with importance sampling -- their composition outperforms pure importance-sampling baselines in our staleness experiments.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [121] [Algebraic Retrieval: Composable Search for Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19482)
*Damian Delmas*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出"代数检索"范式，让AI智能体在查询时以数学查询表达式组合检索策略，并基于PEM验证其执行一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索接口难以让智能体在查询时自由组合相关性准则、资格约束与排序偏好，并难以在查看结果后修订检索程序，因此需要一种可组合、可检验的代数式查询范式。

Method: 在Programmatic Embedding Modulation（PEM）基础上，暴露向量与分数算术运算，构建代数检索查询接口；实现对比评分、候选池重排、加权排序等可组合查询，并提供可执行SQL与PyTerrier对应实现；评估重点为执行一致性而非智能体行为或检索质量。

Result: 在公开的Vaswani数据集（11,429篇文档）上，各程序实现选出相同文档集，分数差异低于1e-6；仅有一对并列文档在不同评分路径下顺序不同。

Conclusion: 代数检索可使查询表面暴露可组合操作，支持智能体按需组合与修订检索程序；PEM在实际数据集上达到高度执行一致性，验证了该范式的可行性。

Abstract: Algebraic Retrieval lets AI agents compose search strategies at query time. Relevance criteria, eligibility constraints, and ranking preferences can be expressed together in a mathematical query. The query surface exposes available operations, so an agent can combine them for the question at hand and revise a program after inspecting results. We evaluate execution parity, not agent behavior or retrieval quality. Building on Programmatic Embedding Modulation (PEM), which exposes vector and score arithmetic during retrieval, we demonstrate contrastive scoring, candidate-pool reranking, and weighted ranking as composable queries, alongside executable SQL and PyTerrier counterparts. On the public 11,429-document Vaswani fixture, each program's implementations select the same document set with score differences below 1e-6; one tied pair orders differently across scoring paths.

</details>


### [122] [Beyond Similarity through Zero-Token Geometric Graphs for Multi-Hop RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19622)
*Zeliang Li,Xiaofen Xing,Kailing Guo,Xiangmin Xu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出G³RAG，一种仅用文档、无需LLM构建图的几何增益图RAG框架，用几何增益分数与密度感知惩罚实现高效多跳证据检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多跳RAG需证据既与查询相关又具足够新颖性以跨越语义鸿沟。稠密检索偏向语义相似文档；图方法依赖昂贵的LLM实体抽取且易沿噪声连接传播。

Method: 离线构图不使用LLM调用或生成token。为每条边赋几何增益分数cosθ·sinθ，同时刻画文档表示间的方向一致性与正交性；采用密度感知拓扑惩罚抑制高连接枢纽节点；从过滤后的查询种子出发进行单步受控扩散，扩展至互补证据。

Result: 在MusiQue、2WikiMultiHopQA、HotpotQA上以Nv-embed-v2与Qwen3-8B-embed评估。两种嵌入设置下，平均F1与答案文档命中率均优于所评估的图基线，平均性能提升最高4.26个F1点，MusiQue上提升5.76点，并消除实体类图方法的构图token开销。

Conclusion: 几何结构可在无需LLM构图的情况下支持高效多跳证据发现。代码已开源。

Abstract: Multi-hop retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) requires evidence that remains relevant to a query while introducing enough novelty to bridge semantic gaps. Dense retrieval tends to concentrate on semantically similar documents, whereas graph-based alternatives often depend on costly Large Language Model (LLM) entity extraction and may propagate through noisy connections. We introduce Geometric Gain Graph RAG (G$^3$RAG), a document-only framework whose offline graph construction uses no LLM calls or generated tokens. G$^3$RAG assigns each edge a geometric gain score, $\cosθ\cdot \sinθ$, that jointly captures directional consistency and orthogonality between document representations. A density-aware topological penalty suppresses highly connected hubs, while single-step controlled diffusion expands from filtered query seeds toward complementary evidence. We evaluate G$^3$RAG on MusiQue, 2WikiMultiHopQA, and HotpotQA using Nv-embed-v2 and Qwen3-8B-embed. G$^3$RAG obtains the best average F1 and answer-document hit rate among the evaluated graph-based baselines in both embedding settings, with gains of up to 4.26 F1 points in average performance and 5.76 points on MusiQue. It also removes the graph-construction token cost incurred by entity-based graph methods. These results show that geometric structure can support efficient multi-hop evidence discovery without LLM-based graph construction. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/G3RAG-99D9/

</details>


### [123] [Self-Evolving Search Index](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19656)
*Sangam Lee,Wonjae Lee,Sunghwan Kim,Deogyong Kim,Jaehoon Kim,Daye Nam,SeongKu Kang,Dongha Lee*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出 SELF-INDEX 框架，让检索索引无需人工干预即可自我演化：Optimizer 自主诊断检索缺陷、选择性修订索引键并验证后再更新，Query Simulator 主动扩展查询需求，跨语料与检索器一致提升检索性能并优于现有索引优化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM agent 需处理多样化信息需求，检索质量高度依赖索引键对文档知识的表达能力；但有效索引表示因检索环境而异，固定优化策略难以稳定生效，而现有索引演化仍以人工为主，需人工诊断失败、调整策略并重处理索引。

Method: SELF-INDEX 框架包含两个核心组件：Optimizer 自主诊断检索短板，选择性修订负有责任的索引键，并在更新索引前对每次修订进行验证；Query Simulator 主动探索额外查询需求，使索引能超越当前可用于优化的查询继续演化。

Result: 在多种语料与检索器上，SELF-INDEX 一致提升检索性能，并优于现有索引优化方法；其收益可延伸至下游应用，提升搜索 agent 的有效性与效率，并帮助 agent 记忆系统检索到有用的历史交互。

Conclusion: 索引可在无人工干预下随检索环境自我演化，且主动模拟查询需求能带来超越已有查询的优化收益，该能力对搜索 agent 与 agent 记忆等下游场景具有实用价值。

Abstract: Information retrieval is increasingly important as LLM agents tackle complex tasks involving diverse information needs. Because retrieval relies on an index that represents each document through index keys, retrieval quality depends heavily on how effectively these keys expose the knowledge contained in each document. However, effective index representations vary across retrieval environments, making it difficult for any fixed optimization strategy to perform consistently. Yet evolving an index to its retrieval environment remains largely human-driven, requiring humans to diagnose retrieval failures, refine the optimization strategy, and reprocess the index accordingly. We propose SELF-INDEX, a framework that enables an index to self-evolve without human intervention. Its Optimizer autonomously diagnoses retrieval shortfalls, selectively revises the responsible index keys, and validates each revision before updating the index. Beyond reacting to observed retrieval demands, SELF-INDEX proactively explores additional demands through a Query Simulator, allowing the index to evolve beyond the queries already available for optimization. Across diverse corpora and retrievers, SELF-INDEX consistently improves retrieval performance while outperforming existing index optimization methods. We further show that these benefits extend to downstream applications, improving the effectiveness and efficiency of search agents and helping agent memory systems retrieve useful past interactions.

</details>


### [124] [Dense Feature Representation over Sequence Modeling: A Solution to the KDD Cup 2026 UniRec Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19787)
*Yi Zhang,Weiliang Ji*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: KDD Cup 2026 腾讯 UniRec 挑战赛（3482万条记录的工业 CVR 预测）第 10 名方案：单变量 15 步链将测试 AUC 从 0.813237 提升至 0.827816，最终提交达 0.828535；消融表明增益主要来自稠密特征表示栈与正交化优化器，序列建模组件贡献可忽略；同时揭示行组切分导致验证 AUC 高估约 0.014 的泛化隐患。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 厘清在大规模工业 CVR 预测中，究竟哪些机制真正提升留出集 AUC。作者指出常规做法容易把注意力放在序列建模的精细化上，而缺乏对"真正驱动指标的因素"的严格验证；此外，官方按行组划分训练/验证集共享同一时间窗口，可能造成验证集指标虚高，需要识别并量化这一泛化风险。

Method: 以官方 PCVRHyFormer 为基线，构建 15 步单变量消融/改进链；对最终模型做 leave-one-out 消融以归因各组件（稠密特征表示栈、正交化优化器、合并单流骨干、极性通道、辅助头、per-token FFN）；并设置时间顺序重划分以获得更可靠的留出评估。

Result: 测试 AUC 由基线 0.813237 升至 0.827816，最终提交 0.828535。留一消融显示：移除稠密特征表示栈损失 0.0095 AUC，移除正交化优化器损失 0.0028；而所有序列建模组件（合并单流骨干、极性通道、辅助头、per-token FFN）的贡献均不超过 0.0005，处于 ±0.0004 的随机种子波动带内。行组式训练/验证切分使验证 AUC 比排行榜高估约 0.014；反记忆与高基数 ID 相关改动在验证集上甚至反号，该分歧源于 dump-to-dump 分布漂移，并在时间有序重划分后依然存在。

Conclusion: 在这一数据规模下，驱动 CVR AUC 的是稠密表示与优化策略，而非更精细的序列建模；结论性判断必须依赖留出的排行榜指标，而非共享时间窗口的验证集。

Abstract: We describe our 10th-place solution to the KDD Cup 2026 Tencent UniRec Challenge, industrial click-to-conversion (CVR) prediction over 34.82M records, and we ask which mechanisms actually move held-out AUC. Starting from the official PCVRHyFormer baseline, a 15-step single-variable chain raises test AUC from 0.813237 to 0.827816, and our final submission reaches 0.828535. A leave-one-out ablation from the full model attributes the gain: removing the dense-feature representation stack costs 0.0095 AUC and removing the orthogonalized optimizer costs 0.0028, while no sequence-modeling component (merged single-stream backbone, polarity channel, auxiliary head, per-token FFN) costs more than 0.0005, within or adjacent to a $\pm$0.0004 seed band. We also report a generalization hazard: the row-group train/validation split shares one time window, so validation AUC overstates the leaderboard by about 0.014; anti-memorization and high-cardinality-ID changes even invert sign against it, a divergence that traces to dump-to-dump distribution shift and survives a time-ordered re-split. Dense representation and optimization, not finer sequence modeling, drive CVR AUC at this scale, and verdicts must come from the held-out leaderboard.

</details>


### [125] [Reproducing Transparent and Scrutable Recommendations: Exploring Open-Weight Models via Natural-Language User Profiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19831)
*Noah Mamié,Laurin van den Bergh*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该可复现性研究验证了基于生成式自然语言用户画像的推荐系统（UPR）的透明度与可审查性，成功复现原始核心结论，并通过消融、多种子稳定性与机制可解释性分析进一步扩展评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始论文提出用生成的用户画像增强推荐系统的透明度与可干预性，但其稳健性与机制证据不足。本文旨在独立复现该结论，并检验画像扰动是否真正影响推荐决策。

Method: 在Amazon Movies & TV、TripAdvisor等数据上复现UPR框架；开展系统化上下文消融实验、在五个随机种子下进行多种子稳定性评估，并使用nnsight框架进行反事实画像扰动下的机制可解释性分析（含激活引导等）。

Result: 复现了原始核心发现：UPR在测试集重排序协议下具有竞争力，且提升透明度。画像扰动会改变预测，但评分变化在各类型间一致，无类型选择性效应，排名也保持不变（即使直接激活引导）。

Conclusion: 该效应源于评分回归目标而非画像接口，因此排序目标模型在该任务上表现明显更优。建议以排序目标为基础设计可审查的画像接口。

Abstract: In this reproducibility study, we investigate the transparency and scrutability of recommender systems enhanced by incorporating generated natural-language user profiles that represent user preferences. The original paper explores the synthesis of user profiles from raw user-generated review text across domains such as movies and accommodations (Amazon Movies & TV, TripAdvisor). Crucially, these natural-language user profiles enable direct user interaction and intervention, allowing users to customize recommendations by correcting misattributed preferences or addressing cold-start settings. We successfully reproduce the core findings of the original study. Additionally, we extend the evaluation by conducting systematic context ablation experiments, multi-seed stability across five distinct random seeds to establish statistical reliability, and a mechanistic interpretability analysis using the nnsight framework to probe internal model representations under counterfactual profile perturbations. Our findings verify the original paper's claim that User Profile Recommendation (UPR) achieves competitive performance under its test-set reranking protocol and makes recommendations more transparent. Perturbing the natural-language profiles does change predictions, but it shifts predicted ratings uniformly across genres with no detectable genre-selective effect, leaving rankings unchanged even under direct activation steering. We trace this back to the rating-regression objective rather than the profile interface, with ranking-objective models clearly exceeding in this task.

</details>


### [126] [The Missing Complement: State-Conditioned Minimal Sufficient Evidence for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20050)
*Zhexi Feng,Ruiyi Zhang,Yongbo Yang,Pengtao Xie*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出"状态条件下的最小充分证据恢复"任务：给定编码智能体的当前状态，恢复能补足其下一步决策仍缺失支撑的紧凑证据集合，而非按段落相关性排序。作者构建SERBench基准（500个状态、45个仓库），并提出MSS-Complement方法，将证据获取建模为集合构造问题（提出充分集→搜索缺口→返回4-8个源单元）。在SERBench上，单一配置（标定数据固定）在5项时恢复完整集合比例达73.0%、8项时80.6%，显著优于Qwen3嵌入+重排（61.4%/72.4%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索器按段落独立打分，而"充分性"是集合属性：排名器可能用同一必需事实的多个变体填满预算，却仍无法支撑决策。智能体在中途任务状态下，其下一步决策需要的是"它还缺什么"，而非"与问题最相似什么"。因此需要重新定义面向智能体的检索问题。

Method: 提出SERBench基准：500个留出状态、45个仓库，记录智能体已见内容，仅当证据集覆盖当前决策所需全部标注事实时才计分。提出MSS-Complement：将证据获取视为集合构造而非排序，通过三次语义调用——(1)提出联合充分集，(2)搜索其缺失部分，(3)返回4-8个完整源单元，总预算6,144 token，配置在标定数据上固定。

Result: 5项时73.0%、8项时80.6%的状态可恢复完整集合，对比Qwen3嵌入+重排为61.4%/72.4%；仅按相似度排序的对照组为66.6%，说明增益来自集合级策略而非计算量。在无金标池的冻结仓库源码上领先5.0个百分点。在AMA-Bench上答案提示缩小76.2%，准确率高出该基准自带记忆智能体2.08个点。从完整集合中移除一个必需组，在两个执行器下修复定位精度分别损失12.3和11.1个点。

Conclusion: 面向智能体的检索应重新表述为"恢复决策所缺证据"而非"对问题相似度重排序"，集合级充分性策略比单纯的相似度排序更有效。

Abstract: A coding agent halfway through an issue has already read much of what a retriever ranks highest. Relevance is scored per passage, but sufficiency belongs to the set: a ranker can fill its budget with variants of one required fact and leave the decision unsupported. We formulate state-conditioned minimal sufficient evidence recovery: given a captured agent state, recover a compact evidence combination that supplies the support its next decision still lacks. SERBench measures this on 500 held-out states from 45 repositories, recording what the agent has seen and crediting only sets that cover every fact the current decision was annotated to require. MSS-Complement treats acquisition as set construction, not ranking. Three semantic calls propose a jointly sufficient set, search for what it lacks, and return 4-8 intact source units within 6,144 tokens. One configuration, fixed on calibration data, recovers a complete set for 73.0% of those states at five items and 80.6% at eight, against 61.4% and 72.4% for Qwen3 embedding with reranking. A matched control ranking by similarity alone reaches 66.6%, placing the gain in the set-level policy, not the computation. From frozen repository source with no gold-derived pool, the lead is 5.0 points. On AMA-Bench it answers from a 76.2% smaller answer prompt, with accuracy 2.08 points above that benchmark's own memory agent. Removing one required group from an otherwise complete set costs 12.3 and 11.1 points of repair-localization precision under two executors. Retrieval for agents is better posed as recovering what a decision lacks than re-ranking what an issue resembles.

</details>


### [127] [Think Thrice Before Reranking: Multi-perspective Evidence and Reasoning Integration for Text Reranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20131)
*Lijun Liu,Zhengzong Chen,Wenyan Li,Yuanyuan Zhao,Fei Huang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出MERIT-Rank框架，通过多视角证据与推理集成改进基于大语言模型的文本重排序，缓解单一推理轨迹带来的错误敏感性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的推理式重排序方法大多依赖单一推理轨迹，导致排序结果易受推理错误影响，且难以建模文档相关性背后的多维信号。

Method: 提出MERIT-Rank框架：构建多轨迹推理空间（MTRS），从多个视角评估查询-文档相关性；引入联合重排序器，将多条推理路径整合为统一排序决策；并设计渐进式排名策略优化（PRPO），通过分阶段优化目标稳定推理轨迹并持续提升排序质量。

Result: 在推理密集型与传统检索基准上，MERIT-Rank均持续优于竞争性基线；其4B模型在BRIGHT上显著超过多数7B甚至32B重排序器。

Conclusion: 多视角推理轨迹建模与渐进式策略优化可有效提升重排序的鲁棒性与性能，并以较小模型规模取得领先效果。

Abstract: Reasoning-based reranking with Large Language Models (LLMs) has shown promising improvements in text ranking. However, current methods predominantly rely on a single reasoning trajectory, resulting in rankings that are susceptible to reasoning errors and inherently constrained in modeling the multifaceted signals underlying document relevance. To resolve this dilemma, we propose MERIT-Rank(Multi-perspective Evidence and Reasoning Integration for Text Reranking), a framework that models complementary reasoning trajectories to improve reranking robustness. MERIT-Rank formulates a Multi-Trajectory Reasoning Space (MTRS) that evaluates query-document relevance from multiple perspectives and introduces a joint reranker that consolidates these reasoning paths into a unified ranking decision. We further develop Progressive Rank Policy Optimization (PRPO), a progressive training framework that stabilizes reasoning trajectories while continually improving ranking quality through staged optimization objectives. Experiments on both reasoning-intensive and traditional retrieval benchmarks show that MERIT-Rank consistently achieves superior performance over competitive baselines. The 4B model notably outperforms most 7B and even 32B rerankers on BRIGHT.

</details>


### [128] [FacetCRS: Multi-Faceted Preference Learning for Pricking Filter Bubbles in Conversational Recommender System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20175)
*Yongsen Zheng,Ziliang Chen,Jinghui Qin,Liang Lin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文提出FacetCRS框架，通过对话式推荐系统中的多面向偏好学习来打破过滤气泡，并在两个公开基准数据集上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过滤气泡是推荐系统中用户仅接触狭窄同质内容、既有偏好被不断强化的现象。现有研究多针对静态或近似静态推荐场景，忽略了真实在线推荐中用户-系统反馈循环会随时间持续加剧过滤气泡的问题。

Method: 提出新范式FacetCRS，在对话式推荐系统（CRS）中借助自然语言的实时用户-物品交互引爆过滤气泡。模型将用户偏好自动建模为多面向，包括实体面向、词面向、上下文面向和评论面向，以捕捉多样且动态的偏好；采用端到端CRS框架，自适应学习各层级偏好面向的表示及多类外部知识。

Result: 在两个公开基准数据集上的大量实验表明，所提方法在缓解过滤气泡和提升CRS推荐质量方面达到当前最优（state-of-the-art）性能。

Conclusion: 通过及时的自然语言对话交互与多面向偏好建模，FacetCRS能够有效打破对话式推荐系统中的过滤气泡，同时提升推荐质量。

Abstract: The filter bubble is a notorious issue in Recommender Systems (RSs), which describes the phenomenon whereby users are exposed to a limited and narrow range of information or content that reinforces their existing dominant preferences and beliefs. This results in a lack of exposure to diverse and varied content. Many existing works have predominantly examined filter bubbles in static or relatively-static recommendation settings. However, filter bubbles will be continuously intensified over time due to the feedback loop between the user and the system in the real-world online recommendation. To address these issues, we propose a novel paradigm, Multi-Facet Preference Learning for Pricking Filter Bubbles in Conversational Recommender System (FacetCRS), which aims to burst filter bubbles in the conversational recommender system (CRS) through timely user-item interactions via natural language conversations. By considering diverse user preferences and intentions, FacetCRS automatically model user preference into multi-facets, including entity-, word-, context-, and review-facet, to capture diverse and dynamic user preferences to prick filter bubbles in the CRS. It is an end-to-end CRS framework to adaptively learn representations of various levels of preference facet and diverse types of external knowledge. Extensive experiments on two publicly available benchmark datasets demonstrate that our proposed method achieves state-of-the-art performance in mitigating filter bubbles and enhancing recommendation quality in CRS.

</details>


### [129] [Reasoning Quality Matters: Combating Reasoning Collapse in LLM-based Embedding Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20563)
*Zihan Gong,Xiaohan Ye,Jiangchao Yao,Jinsong Lan,Xiaoyong Zhu,Xu Chen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出 CoFree 两阶段框架，将 LLM 推理逐步融入查询与文档嵌入优化，同时保持推理质量，缓解"推理坍缩"问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 嵌入方法要么将嵌入学习视为被动的特征提取，要么仅通过指令跟随利用推理能力。向嵌入目标过度专门化会抑制有用的推理生成，或产生与检索无关的文本，作者将这两类退化统称为"推理坍缩"（reasoning collapse）。

Method: 两阶段框架：第一阶段采用参考引导的监督微调（reference-guided SFT），恢复基础嵌入模型的推理能力并保持其表征强度；第二阶段在强化学习中引入双重奖励——面向嵌入的奖励与面向推理的奖励，以保证对嵌入目标相关性的细粒度推理。该端点耦合优化将嵌入学习从静态对齐转变为高质量的推理引导搜索过程。

Result: 在 MTEB 与 BRIGHT 的 22 个数据集上，CoFree-4B 相比 Qwen3-Embedding-4B 平均绝对提升 2.8 个 nDCG@10 点；真实检索系统的在线实验也显示一致增益。

Conclusion: CoFree 有效缓解推理坍缩，显著提升检索嵌入质量；代码、RTED 及模型检查点将公开。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have recently shown strong potential for producing context-rich text embeddings for retrieval. Most existing methods either treat embedding learning as passive feature extraction or exploit LLM reasoning through instruction following for better embedding optimization. However, specialization toward embedding objectives can suppress useful reasoning generation or produce retrieval-irrelevant text. We refer to these two forms of degradation as reasoning collapse. To address this issue, we propose CoFree (Collapse-Free Reasoning Embedding), a two-stage framework that progressively integrates LLM reasoning into query and document embedding optimization while preserving reasoning quality. At the first stage, CoFree applies reference-guided supervised fine-tuning to restore the reasoning ability and retain representational strength of the foundation embedding model. At the second stage, we introduce dual rewards, an embedding-oriented reward and a reasoning-oriented reward, to guarantee fine-grained reasoning of the relevance toward the embedding goal in reinforcement learning. This endpoint-coupled optimization transforms embedding learning from static alignment into a high-quality reasoning-guided search process for retrieval. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of CoFree, with CoFree-4B achieving an average absolute improvement of 2.8 nDCG@10 points over Qwen3-Embedding-4B across 22 datasets from MTEB and BRIGHT. Online experiments in a real-world retrieval system further show consistent gains. Code, RTED, and model checkpoints will be made publicly available.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [130] [Regularized Emphatic Temporal-Difference Learning: Stability under Constant Stepsizes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19170)
*Xingguo Chen,Zhaohui Wu,Jinguo Ye,Chao Li,Shangdong Yang,Guang Yang,Skylar Liang,Wenhao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文构造了一个遍历两状态反例，证明ETD均值映射收缩时采样乘积仍具正最大Lyapunov指数；提出正则化强调式TD（RETD）修复机制，并给出几乎必然收敛及条件性常步长矩收缩结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ETD虽能稳定离策略TD的期望更新并改变投影几何，但这两条性质均不能决定常步长下的采样动态稳定性，因此需要厘清ETD采样动态的真实稳定性机制。

Method: 构造两状态遍历反例，用再生周期分析将Lyapunov指数符号与follow-on迹的无限方差分离；提出RETD，将强调式TD信号存入漏泄标量状态并释放延迟修正，保持迹与重要性比不变，通过单/双正则化读出恢复ETD不动点。

Result: 证明调和递减步长下的几乎必然收敛及基于Markov随机乘积界的条件性常步长矩收缩；RETD在两状态构造和一个Baird点上具有可认证的负指数，而Baird上ETD正号仍为数值结果；10,000次配对实验验证了分离现象、不动点恢复、非单调稳定域与任务依赖性。

Conclusion: RETD改变冲击后动态，但不降低共享的follow-on迹方差；强调式TD的稳定性不能仅由期望更新的收缩性或投影几何判定，采样层面的Lyapunov分析是必要的。

Abstract: Emphatic temporal-difference learning (ETD) stabilizes the expected off-policy TD update and changes its projection geometry, but neither property determines constant-stepsize sampled dynamics. We construct an ergodic two-state counterexample in which the ETD mean map contracts while the sampled product has a positive top Lyapunov exponent. Regenerative-cycle analysis separates this sign from the infinite variance of the follow-on trace. We introduce regularized emphatic TD (RETD), a normalized first-order post-shock repair that leaves the trace and importance ratios unchanged, stores the emphatic TD signal in a leaky scalar state, and releases a delayed correction. RETD's raw equilibrium is an affine shift of the ETD equilibrium; single- and two-regularization readouts recover the ETD fixed point exactly. We prove almost-sure convergence for harmonic diminishing stepsizes and a conditional constant-stepsize moment-contraction result from a Markovian random-product bound. RETD has certified negative exponents on the two-state construction and one Baird point, whereas the positive Baird ETD sign remains numerical. Paired 10,000-run experiments validate both separations, fixed-point recovery, a nonmonotone stability region, and task dependence. RETD changes post-shock dynamics; it does not reduce the shared follow-on-trace variance.

</details>


### [131] [BioPhys-Bridge: A Benchmark for Interdisciplinary Scientific Reasoning in Physics-Grounded Biological Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19180)
*Qingyang Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 BioPhys-Bridge，一个面向生物物理文献证据溯源科学推理的基准数据集，含 500 个案例、1517 个任务，覆盖 6 个生物学领域与 9 类物理模型，并提供初步模型评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型在跨学科科研文献分析中面临独特挑战：生物物理问题需要将观测数据锚定到原始证据、通过定量物理模型进行解释，并关联到生物学机制，而现有基准难以同时覆盖证据溯源、定量推理与机制连接。

Method: 构建 BioPhys-Bridge 基准数据集，每个案例包含证据块、稳定的证据 ID、定量数值、单位、方程、假设、机制以及后续决策，作为 QA 与 RAG 的锚定目标。首批发布 500 个案例、1517 个面向智能体的任务，覆盖 6 个生物学域和 9 个物理模型族（含 3 个为未来扩展保留的稀疏模型族）。所有案例需通过模式、证据完整性、定量锚定、来源许可、去重、单位归一化等严格质量门控，并对 81 个案例进行领域专家评审与标注。

Result: 初步评测显示，DeepSeek-V4-Flash 取得最高证据 ID F1 分数（0.360），其次是 Qwen3.7-Max（0.316）和 GPT-4o-mini（0.294）。

Conclusion: BioPhys-Bridge 是用于评估归因、忠实性、幻觉抑制与生物实验设计的跨学科基准，支持复杂多步科学推理；未来将扩大数据规模与复杂度并开展全面评测。代码与数据已在 GitHub 和 Hugging Face 公开。

Abstract: Language models face unique challenges in analyzing interdisciplinary scientific research literature. In biophysics research, faithful answers require grounding observed data in source evidence, interpreting it through a quantitative physics model, and linking it to a biological mechanism. To address this challenge, we introduce BioPhys-Bridge, a novel benchmark dataset for evidence-grounded scientific reasoning over biophysical literature. Each case contains evidence blocks, stable evidence IDs, quantitative values, units, equations, assumptions, mechanisms, and next decisions as grounding targets for question answering (QA) and retrieval-augmented generation (RAG). The initial release contains 500 cases, 1,517 agent-facing tasks, and covers six biological domains and nine physical model families, including three sparse families reserved for future expansion. We enforce strict quality gates for all cases in schema, evidence-integrity, quantitative-grounding, source-license, duplicate, unit-normalization, with domain expert review and annotation for 81 cases. Preliminary evaluations show that DeepSeek-V4-Flash obtain the highest evidence-ID $F_1$ score (0.360), followed by Qwen3.7-Max (0.316) and GPT-4o-mini (0.294). BioPhys-Bridge is an interdisciplinary benchmark for evaluating attribution, faithfulness, hallucination reduction, and biological experiment design with complex, multi-step scientific reasoning. Future works will increase the size and complexity of the dataset and perform comprehensive evaluations. Code and data are available in the GitHub repository and on Hugging Face.

</details>


### [132] [What Do We Expect from LLMs? Mapping the Design of LLM Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19182)
*Chao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文系统梳理了2022年1月至2026年8月arXiv上14,767篇评测资源论文，通过分阶段筛选与全文自动编码，刻画评测需求（目标系统与领域、评测材料与条件、评分机制）的演变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注模型排行榜本身，难以反映评测需求的变化。作者希望从评测资源（benchmark）的多样性入手，揭示研究者对大语言模型的期待及其对"成功表现"的界定如何演变，并探讨AI参与构建测试、执行任务与评判回答所带来的评测独立性问题。

Method: 收集2022年1月至2026年8月arXiv上14,767篇引入或更新评测资源的论文；采用分阶段筛选（staged screening）与自动化全文编码（automated full-text coding），对目标系统与领域、评测材料与条件、评分机制进行系统编码，并按是否面向智能体（agent）分组比较。

Result: 评测资源日益强调行动、交互与专业应用，同时传统与新近设计要素常并存；模型参与方式发展不均衡——基于LLM的评分在智能体与非智能体群体中均增长，而模型生成材料在近期批次中并未出现持续上升。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了公共研究如何将能力期待转化为具体测试与成功标准。随着AI参与构建测试、执行任务和评判回答，评测的扩展未必带来更多独立证据，反而可能复制参与模型的偏好与盲点，对评测效度与独立性构成挑战。

Abstract: Benchmarks are central to how progress in large language models (LLMs) is assessed and communicated. Yet model rankings alone reveal little about how evaluation requirements themselves are changing. The expanding variety of benchmarks offers another perspective: what researchers expect LLMs to do, and what they count as successful performance. We systematically map 14,767 papers introducing or updating evaluation resources from arXiv submissions between January 2022 and August 2026. Using staged screening and automated full-text coding, we examine changes in target systems and domains, evaluation materials and conditions, and scoring mechanisms. The collection shows growing emphasis on action, interaction, and professional applications, while established and newer design elements frequently coexist. Model participation also develops unevenly: LLM-based scoring grows within both agent and non-agent groups, whereas model-generated materials show no comparable sustained increase in recent cohorts. These findings illuminate how public research translates capability expectations into concrete tests and criteria for success. As AI participates in constructing tests, performing tasks, and judging responses, they also raise a question: does expanding evaluation provide more independent evidence, or risk reproducing the preferences and blind spots of its participating models?

</details>


### [133] [Position: It is Time to Virtualize Foundation Models with a Self-evolving Operating System Layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19203)
*Suparna Bhattacharya,Tarun Kumar,Cong Xu,Satish Kumar Mopur,Jiahao Li,Ashish Mishra,Aalap Tripathy,Annmary Justine Koomthanam,Martin Foltin,Ian Foster*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇立场论文，主张AI应用已从单一基础模型转向复合式智能体系统，但当前技术栈仍高度碎片化。作者提出需要构建"基础模型操作系统"（FMOS），以类似虚拟机抽象物理硬件的方式虚拟化基础模型交互，向上层应用提供专属、可信且能力近乎无界的模型实例，并在内部统一编排记忆层级、模型选择与资源分配、验证与策略执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI系统虽借助MCP、A2A等协议简化了工具与智能体的连接，但各框架各自内置了关于状态、记忆、预算与护栏的隐式运行时，导致行为无法跨框架移植、治理脆弱。这一现状被类比为操作系统出现之前的计算时代——每个程序都要重复实现基础服务，因此亟需一个统一的系统抽象层来标准化与集中这些基础能力。

Method: 作为立场论文，本文主要通过类比论证与架构构想提出FMOS的设计理念，而非实证研究。其核心方法包括：借鉴虚拟机的抽象思想，将FM交互虚拟化为可信、可移植的实例；在内部定义三大编排职能——跨记忆层级的知识编排、模型选择与资源分配、验证与策略执行；并类比人脑"快直觉"与"慢思辨"的双系统切换机制，提出FMOS应学习何时介入、何时让推理直接进行，并基于运行经验持续自适应调整策略。

Result: 本文作为立场性工作，未报告实验数据或量化结果，其"结果"体现为论证观点本身：指出当前碎片化技术栈的根本问题在于缺乏系统层抽象，并论证FMOS作为抽象层能够提升行为可移植性、治理一致性与资源使用效率。文章通过操作系统史与神经科学双系统理论两重类比，为FMOS的合理性与必要性提供了概念性支撑。

Conclusion: 作者得出结论：AI领域需要将FMOS确立为新的系统层，通过虚拟化基础模型交互、统一记忆/模型/资源/验证的编排，并引入自适应的干预策略，来应对复合智能体系统的可移植性与治理难题。FMOS被视为继模型能力与协议连接之后，AI基础设施演进的下一关键环节。

Abstract: AI applications have shifted from single, monolithic foundation models (FM) to compound agentic systems. Yet today's stacks remain fragmented: even as protocols (e.g., MCP, A2A) ease tool/agent connectivity, each framework embeds an implicit runtime for state, memory, budgets, and guardrails, making behavior non-portable and governance brittle. It mirrors computing before operating systems, when every program re-implemented basic services. This position paper argues that the field now needs a Foundation Model Operating System (FMOS) -- a system layer that virtualizes FM interactions analogous to how virtual machines abstract physical hardware, giving applications the illusion of dedicated, trustworthy FM instances with effectively unbounded capabilities. Internally, the FMOS orchestrates knowledge across memory tiers, model selection and resource allocation, and verification and policy enforcement. Like the human brain switching between fast intuition and slow deliberation, the FMOS learns when to intervene and when to let inference proceed directly and continuously adapting its policies based on operational experience.

</details>


### [134] [What Do Current Systematic Generalization Tasks Miss? A Reasoning-Centered Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19212)
*Chengwen Qi,Deheng Ye,Yatao Bian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 现有研究通过线性动作组合、基于生产力的测试和动作显式目标等简化来研究系统性泛化，遗漏了关键要素。作者提出TranSGrid测试平台，将演绎、归纳和溯因推理统一在一个任务中。实验表明，TranSGrid上所有模型表现远低于常规测试集，且任意一种简化都能使解决率回升至测试集水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统性泛化（通过重组已知原子元素解决新问题）是人类智能的核心，但在受控环境下难以严格研究。现有研究依赖线性动作组合、基于生产力的测试、动作显式目标等简化，这些简化虽便于研究，却忽略了该能力的一些本质方面。作者希望刻画这些简化究竟遗漏了什么。

Method: 采用以推理为中心的视角，提出TranSGrid测试平台，在一个统一任务中同时引入演绎、归纳和溯因三种推理。并在4,800个TranSGrid实例上测试七个Transformer模型，同时构造两个变体：一个让动作近似线性组合（降低归纳需求），另一个让目标动作显式化（降低溯因需求），以对照分析简化带来的影响。

Result: 所有模型在TranSGrid上的表现都远差于留出测试集：最大模型在测试集上解决79.6%，但在TranSGrid上仅为55.3%，在最难子集上仅15.8%。性能差距在训练长度范围内依然存在，说明生产力本身不足以评估系统性泛化。当重新引入两种简化中的任意一种后，解决率均回升到大致与测试集持平的水平。

Conclusion: 现有任务至少降低了归纳或溯因需求中的一项；综合衡量系统性泛化需要一个同时涉及演绎、归纳和溯因三种推理形式的任务。

Abstract: Systematic generalization, the ability to solve novel problems by recombining known atomic elements, is central to human intelligence but difficult to study rigorously under controlled settings. Existing studies therefore rely on simplifications such as approximately linear action composition, productivity-based tests, and action-explicit goals, which make systematic generalization easier to study but omit some essential aspects of this capability. To characterize what these simplifications miss, we adopt a reasoning-centered lens and introduce TranSGrid, a testbed that brings deductive, inductive, and abductive reasoning together within a unified task. Experiments with seven Transformers on 4,800 TranSGrid instances show that all models perform much worse on TranSGrid than on a held-out test set: the largest model solves 79.6% of the test set, but only 55.3% of TranSGrid and 15.8% of the hardest subset. The gap remains within the training length range, showing that productivity alone is not sufficient to evaluate systematic generalization. Additionally, we reintroduce the other two simplifications into TranSGrid: one variant makes actions compose almost linearly (reducing the inductive demand), the other makes goals action-explicit (reducing the abductive one). In both, solve rates return to roughly the test set level, showing that either simplification alone is enough to reduce TranSGrid to an ordinary held-out test set. Together, our results show that existing tasks reduce either or both of the inductive and abductive demands, and that comprehensively measuring systematic generalization requires a task that involves all three forms of reasoning.

</details>


### [135] [Characterizing Web Search by Conversational LLM Agents: From Search Decisions and Strategies to Results and Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19244)
*Mahsa Amani,Seungeon Lee,Abhisek Dash,Asmaa El Fraihi,Yunah Jang,Elisabeth Kirsten,Qinyuan Wu,Krishna P. Gummadi,Manish Gupta,Abhilasha Ravichander,Muhammad Bilal Zafar,Soumi Das*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 首个系统研究对话式LLM智能体Web搜索全生命周期（调用决策、查询构建、结果域偏好、结果落地），结合真实用户交互（invivo）与平台API受控实验（invitro），覆盖ChatGPT、Claude、Grok和DeepSeek四大平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对话式LLM智能体日益依赖Web搜索，但其agentic search的端到端生命周期尚缺乏系统理解，因此需要跨平台、跨模型的实证研究来揭示其决策与质量特征。

Method: 采用真实用户交互（invivo）与使用同一平台模型API的受控实验（invitro）相结合的方式，对ChatGPT、Claude、Grok、DeepSeek四个主流对话平台开展Web搜索研究，从四个维度考察：是否调用Web搜索的决策、查询表述策略、所获搜索结果的领域偏好、以及将搜索结果转化为有据回答时的选择。

Result: Web搜索决策在各平台与模型间差异显著；更频繁地调用Web搜索并不必然带来更好的回答质量；智能体采用不同的复杂查询策略；平台特定搜索引擎返回其偏好域的结果；回答虽大体基于搜索结果，但部分论断依赖未引用的搜索结果，引发归属与可靠性担忧。

Conclusion: 研究结果为面向对话式检索优化的未来AI智能体与Web搜索工具设计提供了重要启示。

Abstract: Conversational LLM agents increasingly rely on Web search, yet the end-to-end lifecycle of agentic search remains poorly understood. We present the first study of Web search across four major conversational platforms (ChatGPT, Claude, Grok, and DeepSeek), combining real-world user interactions (invivo) with controlled experiments using the same platform's models by their APIs (invitro). We investigate the quality of agentic decisions to invoke Web search, their strategies to formulate queries, the potential domain preferences in the search results they receive, and the choices they make when transforming search results into grounded responses. We find that Web-search decisions vary substantially across platforms and models, while more frequent Web-search invocation does not necessarily yield better response quality. We further show that conversational agents employ different complex querying strategies and that platform specific search engines return search results from their preferred domains. Finally, although responses are largely grounded in search results, some claims rely on uncited search results, raising concerns about attribution and reliability. Our findings have important implications for the design of future AI agents and Web search tools optimized for conversational retrieval.

</details>


### [136] [Do AI Agents Understand Computer Architecture?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19387)
*Ambika Sharan,Grigory Chirkov,Soheil Abbasloo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AutoTuring 通过对同一智能体施加"有语义命名"与"匿名变量"两套等价问题框架，首次将"智能体是否真正理解硬件"从"智能体是否擅长搜索"中分离出来，并用两者的性能差作为可测量指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作普遍报告智能体能够设计并改进硬件加速器，但这类结果只能证明设计变好了，无法说明改进的来源。智能体可能真的在对机器进行架构层面的推理，也可能只是在从未理解其含义的旋钮空间上做高效搜索；只有前者才能迁移到下一个架构。既有评测无法区分二者，因为它们固定问题框架、只变化智能体本身。

Method: 本文反其道而行，固定智能体、变化问题框架。AutoTuring 将同一个智能体两次置于同一个 15 维加速器设计空间：一次以带名称的架构旋钮加模拟器计数器形式呈现，一次以 [0,1] 上的匿名变量形式呈现；两次的评估器、合法搜索空间与可达最优解完全一致，唯一变量是问题是否"有含义"。两者之间的性能差距即为所测量的量。实验在九核 FP16 GEMM 任务集上进行。

Result: 在九核 FP16 GEMM 任务集上，"语义"带来收益：架构智能体平均比建模的 H200 高 5.4%，比其盲搜索对照高 12.3%，且模拟器调用次数少 70.1%。但语义收益并非不可替代：加入 critic 循环后，盲智能体弥补了大部分差距，而架构智能体未获额外增益，说明架构知识与结构化批判在此表现为替代关系而非互补关系。

Conclusion: 作者将结论定位为初步发现（每条件 5–6 次运行、单一建模加速器），并强调贡献在于该对比方法论本身，而非所设计的加速器。该框架为区分"理解驱动的改进"与"纯搜索驱动的改进"提供了可控的测量手段。

Abstract: Agents are increasingly asked to design hardware, and increasingly reported to succeed. Such reports establish that a design improved; they cannot establish why. An agent that improves an accelerator may be reasoning about the machine, or may be searching competently over knobs whose meaning it never recovers -- and only the first transfers to the next architecture. Existing evaluations cannot tell the two apart, because they vary the agent while holding the framing of the problem fixed. We do the opposite. AutoTuring hands the same agent the same 15-dimensional accelerator space twice: once as named architectural knobs with simulator counters, once as anonymous variables on [0,1], with the evaluator, the legal space and the reachable optima held identical, so that the only thing that varies is whether the problem means anything. The gap between the two is the measurement. On a nine-kernel FP16 GEMM basket, meaning pays: the architect beats a modeled H200 by 5.4% and its blind counterpart by 12.3% on average, with 70.1% fewer simulator calls. It does not pay uniquely: a critic loop recovers most of that gap for the blind agent and buys the architect nothing, so architectural knowledge and structured critique behave as substitutes rather than as complements. We report these as preliminary findings -- five to six runs per condition on a single modeled accelerator -- and take the comparison itself, not the accelerator, to be the contribution.

</details>


### [137] [MAGS: Multi-agent Auto-formalization Guarantees Safety for Agentic Outputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19391)
*Albert Wu,Nicholas Roberts,Tzu-Heng Huang,Haoran Lin,Gil Friedman,Sungjun Cho,Gabriel Orlanski,Frederic Sala*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MAGS多智能体框架，以Dafny为验证中间表示，将LLM生成代码自动形式化验证并修复，在220个任务上实现100%非平凡安全保证成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM编码智能体生成的复杂程序规模庞大，人工审查难以全面覆盖，易残留安全与安全隐患。模糊测试、静态分析与LLM-as-a-Verifier虽能发现部分缺陷，却难以覆盖所有边界情况。形式化验证可提供机器可检查的保证，但传统上依赖大量人工规格编写与证明工程，成本过高。

Method: MAGS是一个统一多智能体框架：形式化并冻结经人工审核的API与安全需求；将生成代码翻译为Dafny这一验证感知中间表示；利用验证器反馈自动修复违规；再将验证通过的程序编译回可执行代码，从而产出带形式化安全保证的可执行程序。

Result: 在100个CUDA内核、100个终端脚本和20个机械臂任务共220个样例上，MAGS针对冻结规格实现100%成功率，产出具备非平凡安全保证的程序。独立的安全性与功能性评估显示三个领域均表现强劲，同时暴露出自动形式化语义未能完全刻画目标行为时的失败情形。

Conclusion: MAGS表明多智能体+验证感知中间表示的路线可在无需大规模人工证明工程的前提下，为LLM生成程序提供可机检的安全保证。其局限在于自动形式化语义与目标行为之间的差距会导致失败，这提示语义抽取质量是后续改进的关键方向。

Abstract: LLM coding agents now generate complex programs at a scale that makes thorough human review increasingly difficult, raising the risk of safety and security failures. Common approaches, including fuzz testing, static analysis, and LLM-as-a-Verifier, can detect many failures but struggle to cover all possible edge cases. Formal verification addresses this by providing machine-checkable guarantees over specified properties, but traditionally demands substantial manual specification and proof engineering. We introduce a unified multi-agent framework, MAGS, that generates executable programs with formal safety guarantees, using Dafny as a verification-aware intermediate representation where safety properties can be mechanically checked. MAGS formalizes and freezes human-audited APIs and safety requirements, translates generated code into Dafny, repairs violations using verifier feedback, and compiles verified programs back into executable code. We evaluate MAGS on 100 CUDA kernels, 100 terminal scripts, and 20 robotic-arm tasks. Across all 220 examples, it achieves a 100% success rate in producing programs with non-trivial safety guarantees against frozen specifications. Independent safety and functional evaluations further show strong performance across all three domains, while revealing failures when the auto-formalized semantics do not fully capture the target behavior.

</details>


### [138] [Closed-World Resolution Against Tool Hallucination in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19425)
*Laxmipriya Ganesh Iyer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文指出工具增强型LLM智能体存在被现有工具选择与工具安全方法忽视的结构性盲区——调用不存在的工具或传递未声明的参数，并对此开展测量与基准研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御分两类：工具选择（选对工具）与门控（约束智能体对真实工具的行为），二者都预设所发出的调用至少指向一个真实工具。而幻觉调用在构造上并非任何门控作出的决策，因此任何门控都无法拒绝它，形成结构性盲区，且该问题此前缺乏系统测量。

Method: 提出五类工具幻觉分类法（H1–H5）与一个训练无关、闭世界的解析器Resolution Rung（注册表成员检查加签名校验），其关注点在于应置于何处而非计算内容；证明幻觉防御必须前置任何因果门控，并刻画唯一不可约残余（借用参数，其模式与合法调用无法区分）。在十种托管模型、两种调用界面上测量；进一步扩展到模型上下文协议（MCP），给出第二套分类法M1–M5。

Result: 两种调用界面共测得322例真实幻觉；虚构工具调用集中于无约束的原始JSON界面（34对3），且模型规模无帮助（675B与7–8B持平）。在MCP实际界面上测得154例幻觉，其中包含在单注册表界面上表现干净的前沿模型，原因是合并导致的名字冲突与遮蔽是结构性的。

Conclusion: 幻觉防御在架构上必须先于因果门控；多服务器命名空间合并会引入单一注册表无法表达的新幻觉界面。论文发布带版本的幻觉工具基准（HTB），使任何解析器可在不同提交间进行可比评测。

Abstract: Tool-augmented large language model (LLM) agents fail in a way no tool-selection or tool-security method addresses: they call tools that do not exist and pass arguments no schema declares. Existing defenses either pick the right tool (selection) or constrain what an agent may do with real tools (gating), both of which presuppose the emitted call refers to a real tool at all. We show this is a structural blind spot: a hallucinated call is by construction not a decision any gate made, so no gate can reject it. This paper is primarily a measurement and benchmark study. We give a five-class taxonomy of tool hallucination (H1-H5) and, as a reference point, the Resolution Rung: a training-free, closed-world resolver (registry membership plus a signature check) whose interest is where it must sit, not what it computes. We prove hallucination defense must precede any causal gate, and characterize the one irreducible residue (borrowed arguments schema-indistinguishable from a valid call). Across ten hosted models under two invocation surfaces we measure 322 genuine hallucinations; fabricated-tool calls concentrate on the unconstrained raw-JSON surface (34 vs. 3), and model scale does not help (a 675B model matches a 7-8B one). We then extend to the Model Context Protocol, where merging several servers into one namespace creates hallucination surfaces a single registry cannot express (a second taxonomy, M1-M5); on the live MCP surface we measure 154 hallucinations, including from frontier models that were clean on the single-registry surface, because collisions and shadowing are structural to the merge. We release the versioned Hallucinated-Tools Benchmark (HTB) so any resolver is comparable across submissions.

</details>


### [139] [The syntax and semantics of goals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19448)
*David M. Abel,Mark K. Ho*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文将"目标"视为可组合的表征，主张借鉴语言学与逻辑学中句法-语义界面的视角，把目标表征的形式与意义区分开来，并综合相关研究刻画不同目标表征的性质，将其视为一个更广阔"设计空间"中的不同坐标点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 认知科学与计算机科学通常把目标理解为与知识结合以组织目的性行为的认知状态，但多将其视为固定且施加约束的实体，忽视了对目标表征本身属性（如表达力、设计、效率）的基础性追问。作者希望借助目标与语言学、逻辑学中表征-内容关系的类比，凸显这些被忽略的问题。

Method: 概念分析与理论综合：通过类比句法-语义界面，区分目标表征的"形式"与"意义/内容"；提出并刻画目标表征本身所受的约束（如某种目标语言是否足够表达所关注的行为、不同目标表征是否刻画同一行为）；综合既有研究以勾勒目标表征的设计空间。

Result: 论文并非报告实证数据，而是提出分析框架：识别出目标表征在表达力、设计与效率等方面的性质构成一个更广泛的设计空间，并表明不同目标表征可视为该空间中的不同点。

Conclusion: 区分目标的形式与意义有助于澄清关于目标的隐含假设、促进对高层认知与动机之间交互的研究，并为不同目标概念分离出变异维度。

Abstract: In both cognitive science and computer science, goals are conceptualized as cognitive states that flexibly combine with world knowledge to organize and specify purposeful behavior. In this way, goals are compositional representations whose content relates to rational behavior. We here draw attention to goals as representations and their content because it highlights a parallel with other areas in cognitive science - in particular, the syntax-semantics interface in linguistics and logic - while also foregrounding foundational questions about the expressivity, design, and efficiency of different goal representations.
